Correlation: these models develop alignable representational geometry across layers, with alignment strengthening as data scale, model scale, or linguistic proximity increases. Construction: a single Procrustes rotation fit on parallel sentences maps hidden states between models. Causation: the same rotation transfers functional content; patching a rotated English residual into a German model on a factual cloze flips the prediction to the donor's capital in most cases. We confirm that cross-lingual alignment can emerge from the structure of language and the information it carries rather than from joint training, and this points to practical future directions including model stitching, merging, and modular multilingual systems built from monolingual components.

</details>


### [184] [TwinKV: A Composable Repair Pass for KV Cache Eviction via Pairwise Key Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27128)
*Hong Chen,Yudong Zeng,Yongwei Huang,Zuhao Ouyang,Junyan Zhang,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TwinKV方法，用于提升长上下文推理中关键值缓存（KV cache）的使用效率，并验证其在多种现有缓存淘汰策略上的增益效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理受限于KV cache的内存开销，尤其是在小模型和资源有限的情况下。目前主流的KV cache淘汰方法依赖于注意力分布对token进行评分，但作者通过实验发现这种评分与token对最终答案的贡献无关，因此需要探索更合理的缓存管理方法。

Method: 提出了TwinKV，这是一种无需训练、与注意力无关的冗余检测信号，用以判断上下文中某token的key是否有近似重复。如果有些被淘汰的token在保留的集合中没有重复（“孤儿”），而保留的token的信息在其他地方有重复（“冗余捐赠者”），则进行交换，既不改变原有评分和缓存空间，又提升缓存信息的有效性。TwinKV可作为模块组合到多种已有淘汰策略中。

Result: 在LongBench、LooGLE、RULER和MMLU-Pro等数据集上，将TwinKV与四种现有淘汰策略组合，在不同压缩比下测试。以Qwen3-4B为例，TwinKV对其中两种策略大部分配置有提升，对第三种基本无影响，对第四种（本已接近性能上限）只能小幅提升。RULER和Llama-3.2-1B上，第四种策略在所有情况下都提升。Llama-3.2-1B总体提升幅度较小，但在单元格数比例上表现更优。然而，在few-shot分类类任务上，TwinKV作用有限。

Conclusion: 当前主流的KV cache淘汰评分方法存在局限，TwinKV提出了基于冗余的新型修正机制，在多种实际配置下都能提升模型在有限缓存空间下的表现，但其有效性也依赖具体任务结构。

Abstract: Long-context inference is bottlenecked by the memory footprint of the key-value (KV) cache, especially for small models under tight resource budgets. Existing KV cache eviction methods score tokens using the model's attention distribution or, in attention-free variants, each key's distance from a global reference point. Using a controlled leave-one-out probe, we find that attention magnitude is unrelated to a token's causal contribution to the answer (Spearman $ρ=-0.004$), challenging the premise behind dominant eviction methods. We introduce TwinKV, a training-free, attention-free redundancy signal that detects whether a token's key has a near-duplicate elsewhere in context. Rather than replacing existing policies, TwinKV acts as a composable repair pass: given a policy's fixed retained set, it identifies evicted tokens with no surviving duplicate (\emph{orphans}) and retained tokens whose information is duplicated elsewhere (\emph{redundant donors}), then swaps them while preserving the original budget and scoring rule. We compose TwinKV with four recent eviction policies across LongBench, LooGLE, RULER, and a short-context MMLU-Pro no-harm control at compression ratios ${0.3,0.5,0.7}$. On Qwen3-4B, TwinKV improves a majority of configurations for two policies, is near-even for a third, and helps only a minority for a fourth adaptive baseline already near a performance ceiling; gains across the three non-ceiling policies are smallest at the loosest ratio. On RULER with Llama-3.2-1B, however, that fourth policy improves in every evaluated cell because its Alone score leaves substantial room to improve. More broadly, Llama-3.2-1B shows a smaller average LongBench gain but a higher fraction of improved cells on LongBench and LooGLE than Qwen3-4B, plus a clean RULER win. We also identify few-shot classification exemplars as a task structure where TwinKV does not help on either model.

</details>


### [185] [Said Aloud, Read Different: Cross-Modal Instability in Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27135)
*Basel Mousi,Fahim Dalvi,Shammur Chowdhury,Firoj Alam,Nadir Durrani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 作者提出了一个结合语音与视觉的新基准，用于评估多模态大模型在理解口语与文本查询方面的一致性，特别聚焦于英语与阿拉伯语。该研究发现，模态与语言的变化会显著增加模型判断的不一致性，且这种不一致性并不能被传统整体准确率完全反映。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型越来越多应用于需要理解口语并作出视觉相关决策的助手系统，但目前仍不清楚模型在不同输入模态（文本vs语音）和语言（英语vs阿拉伯语）下，能否保持一致判断。该研究关注于语音助手系统在跨文化、跨模态、跨语言环境中的稳定性。

Method: 作者构建了一个新的多模态三元组对比基准，包含来自18个中东和北非国家的10,150张文化相关图片，每张图片配有一句支持语句和两个看似合理但无事实支持的替代语句。提出对比不稳定性指标，并在英语与阿拉伯语的文本及语音模态下，评测了当前多模态模型表现。

Result: 评测显示，输入模态（语音vs文本）和语言的切换会导致三元组判断上的显著不一致性，表现为部分推理失败。这种不一致性不会被传统总准确率指标捕捉，而语音输入会加剧这种局部失败。

Conclusion: 当前多模态基础模型在处理多语种、多模态输入时存在明显稳定性短板。研究团队公开了该基准数据集，希望推动社区在跨模态、跨语言场景下的鲁棒性研究。

Abstract: Multimodal foundation models are increasingly used in speech-first assistants that must interpret spoken queries and produce visually grounded decisions. Yet it remains unclear whether semantically equivalent queries yield consistent judgments across modality (text vs. speech) and language (English vs. Arabic). We introduce a speech-augmented visually grounded contrastive triplet benchmark spanning 10,150 culturally grounded images from 18 MENA countries, where each image is paired with one supported statement and two plausible but unsupported alternatives. We define contrastive instability as the conditional rate at which a model fails to resolve all statements within a triplet, isolating fragmented reasoning from complete failure. Evaluating recent multimodal models under text and speech in English and Arabic, we find that modality and language shifts introduce substantial triplet-level inconsistencies that are not fully captured by aggregate accuracy, with speech amplifying partial failures. We make the benchmark publicly available to the community.

</details>


### [186] [STAR : Sentence Translation Alignment Rate for Document-to-Document Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27161)
*Yichen Dong,Hao Wang,Junhui Li,Linlong Xu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出STAR和StarPO框架，有效提升大模型文档级翻译的结构一致性和质量，使小模型在翻译任务上超越GPT-4o等大型闭源系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档级（Doc2Doc）翻译往往出现句子遗漏或虚构句子等结构错位问题，影响译文与原文的对应关系。当前缺乏专门用于衡量和优化结构一致性的有效指标和方法。

Method: 提出句子翻译对齐率（STAR）指标，量化句子级结构保真度；设计STAR-masked Preference Optimization（StarPO）框架，通过结构质量对多种翻译假设进行排序，并用动态对齐掩码聚焦优化结构错位的片段。

Result: 在新闻和文学领域实验证明，StarPO显著提升翻译质量和结构完整性。小规模模型经过StarPO优化后，在性能和Token效率上均超过GPT-4o等大型闭源系统。

Conclusion: StarPO方法可有效提升文档级翻译的结构一致性，使紧凑模型在实际应用中具备超越大型模型的潜力，对机器翻译和大模型生成任务具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled a shift from sentence-level to document-to-document (Doc2Doc) machine translation, promising improved global coherence. However, document-to-document generation in a single pass frequently suffers from structural misalignment, manifesting as sentence omissions or hallucinations that violate the core requirement of source-target correspondence. To address this, we introduce Sentence Translation Alignment Rate (STAR), an auxiliary metric that explicitly quantifies sentence-level structural fidelity. Building on this, we propose STAR-masked Preference Optimization (StarPO), a framework that ranks document-level hypotheses by structural quality and utilizes a dynamic alignment mask to focus optimization on misaligned segments. Experimental results across news and literary domains demonstrate that StarPO significantly enhances translation quality and structural integrity. Notably, StarPO allows compact models to surpass the performance of massive proprietary systems like GPT-4o while maintaining superior token efficiency.

</details>


### [187] [Prediction of Prediction (PoP): Inter-Layer Activation Fusion for Single-Pass Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27165)
*Himal Badu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Prediction of Prediction (PoP)的新机制，通过对大语言模型生成时的中间隐状态进行分析，判断生成内容的事实正确性。该方法可在一次前向计算内完成，极大减少了额外资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型在生成文本时会输出自信但实际上并不准确的信息，传统的不确定性度量方法对模型的过度自信无能为力，多采样验证又带来较大的计算和延迟开销。因此，需要开发更高效且有效的方法辅助判断模型输出的事实正确性。

Method: 作者提出了PoP机制，通过融合模型在生成过程中的各层中间隐状态，捕捉层与层之间的不确定性变化。在只需一次前向传播的基础上，分析这些变化，以判断最终输出的可靠性，无需额外的解码调用或多次生成采样。

Result: 在TruthfulQA基准上评测，PoP机制在事实正确性分类任务上表现出较好效果，AUROC达到75.5%。同时，该机制让推理延时仅增加1.2%，且无需增加额外生成过程。

Conclusion: PoP方法能高效地利用模型内部信号判断输出的事实正确性，降低额外开销，有助于提升大模型在关键场景下的应用可靠性。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language models (LLMs) routinely generate factually incorrect outputs with high decoding confidence, limiting their deployment in high-stakes workflows. Existing output-stage uncertainty metrics can fail when models are overconfident on false assertions, while multi-sample verification pipelines introduce substantial memory and latency overhead. This work evaluates whether internal hidden-state transition dynamics during generation can signal factual errors without auxiliary decoding calls. We introduce Prediction of Prediction (PoP), a mechanism that captures layer-transition uncertainty by fusing intermediate hidden representations across depth during a single forward pass. Evaluated on the TruthfulQA benchmark using autoregressive transformer backbones, PoP achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 75.5% for factual-correctness classification. The mechanism operates within the base forward pass, adding less than 1.2% runtime latency and requiring zero additional generation passes. The numerical results are reported from the author-verified experimental implementation and are bounded by the evaluation scope described below.

</details>


### [188] [When Text Misleads: Inconsistent-Aware Reasoning for Audio-Grounded Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27176)
*Yen-Ju Lu,Yuzhe Wang,Yaohan Guan,Xiluo He,Jiarui Hai,Mingrui Liang,Kaavya Chaparala,Thomas Thebaud,Laureano Moro-Velazquez,Najim Dehak,Jesus Villalba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的基准ContraTalk，揭示了语音对话理解中模型过分依赖文本内容的弊端，并通过创新方法提升模型在音频信号上的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话理解评估常允许模型仅凭文本内容或单一模态取得高分，忽略了语音中的重要辅助手段（如情感、对话意图等），导致模型可能未真正利用语音信息进行推理。作者希望识别并解决这种依赖文本的“捷径”式错误。

Method: 1) 正式定义“跨模态分歧”问题，筛选出仅凭文本会引发误解的样本，建立包含分歧和一致两类问答的ContraTalk数据集（501题，涵盖5类对话属性）；2) 提出Audio Twin方法，将局部音频线索转化为可读文本，为推理模型提供明确音频证据。

Result: 实验证明：1) 文本大模型在一致案例准确率超90%，遇到“分歧”样本时骤降至33-48%；2) 直接音频大模型部分改善，但有30-40%仍选错（陷入文本陷阱）；3) Audio Twin方法在分歧案例中准确率提升，并且陷阱选择减少。

Conclusion: 语音对话理解中，模型依赖文本进行捷径推理是重要失效模式。显式聚合和暴露音频证据有助于更好诊断和增强模型的语音推理能力。

Abstract: Understanding spoken dialogue requires joint reasoning over lexical content and paralinguistic acoustic signals such as emotion and conversational intent. However, existing evaluations often allow shortcuts based on transcripts or single-modality solutions, obscuring whether models genuinely ground predictions in speech. We formalize this failure mode as cross-modal disagreement, where transcripts suggest plausible but incorrect surface interpretations while acoustic cues such as prosody or speaking style support different answers. We develop a scalable framework that identifies text-biased surface interpretations and converts disagreement regions into conflict QA examples. We also include consistent cases where transcript-based and speech-grounded interpretations agree, enabling evaluation beyond adversarial audio dependence. This results in ContraTalk, a controlled benchmark containing 501 questions across five discourse dimensions: interaction behavior, emotion state, dialogue act, social stance, and conversational intent. We further develop an agentic-style reasoning framework that converts speech into an Audio Twin, a text-readable representation of localized acoustic cues that exposes acoustic evidence to the reasoning model. Experiments show that strong text-only LLMs exceed 90% accuracy in consistent cases but drop to 33-48% in conflict cases. Direct AudioLLMs provide only partial grounding, still selecting the transcript-biased trap in roughly 30-40% of conflict cases. Our Audio Twin framework improves conflict-case accuracy while reducing trap selection, but its consistent-case behavior remains backbone-dependent. These results identify transcript-based shortcuts as an important failure mode in spoken dialogue understanding and show that explicit acoustic evidence aggregation provides a more controllable interface for diagnosing and improving speech-grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [189] [BALMS: Benchmarking Agentic LLMs for Longitudinal Mental Health Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27219)
*Yu Yvonne Wu,Arvind Pillai,Yuliang Chen,Yuwei Zhang,Sudarshan Regmi,Tess Z. Griffin,Michael V. Heinz,Lisa A. Marsch,Nicholas C. Jacobson,Andrew Campbell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BALMS，这是首个系统性基准，专为基于大模型（LLM）的智能系统进行长期心理健康感知能力评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前心理健康评估依赖于自评量表，这种做法只能获得零散的数据点。可穿戴设备能够连续获取行为和生理信号，有潜力实现持续、低负担的心理健康监测。最近LLM驱动的健康助理仅限于短期数据查询，缺乏对长期信号推理、预测和证据支撑的能力，因此需要一个系统性基准来推动领域发展。

Method: 作者提出BALMS基准，用于系统评测LLM agent在长期心理健康感知上的表现。涵盖3个真实纵向数据集、2类任务（分数预测和由LLM判定的推理生成）、5种（开源/闭源）LLM agent，并比较多种启发式与推理范式的效果。

Result: 零样本能力的agent一般难以超过均值基线，除非有更强大的模型或高质量特征。链式思维提升了特定模型的推理，但难以保证时间证据溯源或数值正确性。

Conclusion: BALMS揭示现有LLM agent在长期心理健康监控场景中的局限，同时指出了未来需要关注高效、时间溯源与可解释性推理的方向。

Abstract: Mental health assessment relies on episodic self-report scales, which convert subjective states such as stress into numerical scores but provide only sparse snapshots of wellbeing. Wearable devices offer longitudinal behavioral and physiological signals for continuous, low-burden monitoring. Recent LLM-driven personal-health agents enable natural language queries over wearable signals, but mainly handle short-term, retrieval-based lookups (e.g., highest step count over a week). They do not evaluate whether agents can reason over long-term signals to predict wellbeing scores paired with evidence-grounded rationales. To address this gap, we introduce BALMS, the first systematic benchmark of LLM-based agentic systems for longitudinal mental health sensing. BALMS spans 3 real-world longitudinal datasets, 2 task families (closed-form wellbeing-score prediction and rationale generation auto-graded by an LLM-as-Judge), 3 agentic paradigms evaluated across 5 open- and closed-source LLM backbones. We find that zero-shot agents rarely outperform a simple mean baseline, except with stronger backbones or compact, semantically meaningful features. Chain-of-thought prompting improves reasoning-oriented backbones, but does not guarantee temporal grounding or numerical correctness. Together with more analysis on efficiency and temporal scaling, BALMS highlights the need for longitudinal mental health agents that selectively retrieve history, ground temporal evidence, and reason over interpretable behavioral features.

</details>


### [190] [SCIT: Testing Causal Cache Carriers in Latent Chain-of-Thought Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27265)
*Yi Ding,Lijun Huang,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SCIT的新方法，用于研究变换器模型（如GPT-2）中链式思维的因果机制，发现主要通过缓存值的后缀传递算术信息，而非隐藏状态等其他机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着链式思维（CoT）方法的发展，推理信息逐渐内化为模型的连续状态，但这导致因果机制变得不透明，难以理解模型内部是如何进行推理和算术操作的。为解决这一问题，需要新的实验工具来揭示模型的中间因果对象。

Method: 提出Suffix Cache Interchange Test（SCIT）协议，通过精确构建源-收件人反事实、修补声明缓存段，采用充分性测试、键/值分拆、隐藏状态控制、语义源控制、解码验证和匹配破坏等多种测试手段，分析不同模型及检查点下算术信息的传递方式。

Result: 在CODI-GPT2和Sim-CoT风格复现模型中，算术信息主要通过Value-cache后缀轨迹传递，不依赖于隐藏状态或特定token。主CODI-GPT2检查点能完整支持这一机制，但Sim-CoT模型在必要性证据上不足。扩展到更大模型（如8B参数）时，机制会发生迁移，通过前缀或全缓存传递，而有些边界单元则无机制可判定。

Conclusion: SCIT为分析语言模型缓存级别的因果机制提供了新工具，阐明了不同模型和参数规模下的算术信息承载方式，为理解和诊断Transformer模型提供了重要参考，否定了“普适缓存尾部”机制的假设。

Abstract: Latent chain-of-thought models move intermediate reasoning from emitted text into continuous states, improving compactness but hiding the causal object. We introduce SCIT, the Suffix Cache Interchange Test, a causal protocol that constructs exact source-recipient counterfactuals, patches declared cache segments, and identifies which transformer object carries the counterfactual computation. SCIT combines sufficiency tests with K/V component splits, hidden-state controls, semantic source controls, decoded validation, and matched corruption. On CODI-GPT2 and a Sim-CoT-style GPT-2 reproduction, counterfactual arithmetic transfers primarily through value-cache suffix trajectories rather than hidden states, keys, reusable answer slots, or single-token triggers. Complete sufficiency-and-necessity evidence for the late-value-suffix mechanism holds for the main CODI-GPT2 checkpoint; the Sim-CoT-style checkpoint shows the same sufficiency and decoded-control pattern but insufficient matched-corruption evidence for a necessity call. Beyond these local arithmetic cells, SCIT reveals carrier-regime shifts: arithmetic-like GPT-2/1B cells preserve latent-tail value/KV transfer, whereas competent 8B and repaired non-arithmetic cells route through prompt-prefix or full-cache K/V; boundary cells receive no mechanism call. SCIT therefore contributes a cache-level diagnostic, a checkpoint-specific GPT-2 arithmetic mechanism, and a competence-gated carrier map rather than a universal latent-tail claim.

</details>


### [191] [Difference-in-Differences on a Censored Rating Scale Can Manufacture an Effect: Evidence from a Pre-Registered LLM-Judge Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27309)
*Shuyi Fan,Boyuan Deng,Mengyu Xu,Xinhong Xie,Chenyang Li,Hongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对大语言模型（LLM）评判结果进行审计，发现当前广泛采用的对比审计设计会混淆偏好差异和评价严苛度变化，导致偏差。进一步实验证明，在实际操作中此类统计结果并不能有效反映真实偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，LLM广泛用于评判任务，其判断准则是否公正客观备受关注。为检测偏见，研究者采用双重对比设计。然而，该方法是否真正有效揭示偏好存在争议。

Method: 本文解析并推导双重对比（double difference）审计设计的数学机制，并在一个封存、预注册的LLM评判实验中实证检验：操控学习者侧写观察评委偏好变化，同时跟踪评分在量表上的分布和极限效应。

Result: 实证结果显示操控变量（学习者侧写）对偏好无显著影响（+0.085，p=0.684），唯一显著的交互项也可以在无任何真实偏好差异情况下，仅由评分分布机制和量表边界产生79-85%的显著统计效应。

Conclusion: 现有的双重对比审计方式存在结构性混淆，单凭评分量表无法鉴别真实偏好和尺度极限效应。本文推导出识别机制，并证明这一偏差属于方法本身，可由审计评分数据自行测量与校正。

Abstract: Audits of LLM judges certify a bias by contrasting matched conditions, and the strongest designs difference twice: a within-item contrast between two candidate responses, differenced again across a manipulated attribute, read off a bounded rating scale. We show that this endpoint is not identified on the scale that reports it. Each term of the double difference is censored by its own share, so the observed statistic confounds differential preference with differential attenuation: a severity shift common to both responses manufactures an interaction whenever the two censor it unequally, as unequal distances from the bounds make them, exactly where good stimuli place them. We exhibit the failure inside a pre-registered audit of a frozen pedagogy judge, sealed before the first of its 990 calls. The registered primary endpoint, the effect of a stated learner profile on the judge's scaffolding preference, is null: $+0.085$ points (95\% BCa $[-0.167, +0.353]$, $p = 0.684$). The audit's one nominally significant interaction, $+0.378$ ($p = 0.002$), is not identified as preference: a construction containing zero differential preference reproduces 79 to 85\% of it from the observed severity shift and the scale floor alone. We derive the mechanism in closed form and show that its contribution is measurable from an audit's own ratings.

</details>


### [192] [BTS-AgentBench: A Deterministic, Replayable Pipeline from Read-Only Telemetry Logs to Agent Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27334)
*Jeong-Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从工业遥测数据自动构建多轮智能体任务（BTS-AgentBench）的方法，并开源了相关工具、数据和报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业现场有大量只读遥测数据，但缺乏将这些数据编译成可执行多轮智能体任务的标准方法和基准，限制了智能体任务开发与评测。

Method: 开发了一个流水线，将BTS元数据和原始历史标准化为只读工具保存库，编译出包含工具-派生的标准答案及证据的静态任务，并进一步转化为类型化、带约束、面向操作员的任务集。通过构建排除和质量门控机制确保任务质量，一致性经多次构建验证。

Result: 发布了包含532项的新数据集，完善了澄清、目标修订、时间策略等要素，保证了数据溯源与合理划分；构建流程在多个数据集上表现出高可复现性与无异常；对应GPT-5.5的自动执行表现为全部通过，代码与数据已开源。

Conclusion: 所提出的自动化流水线拓展了工业遥测数据向智能体任务转化的能力，为相关研究与应用提供了高质量的标准基准和工具。

Abstract: Industrial sites contain large volumes of read-only telemetry, but few benchmarks specify how to compile these records into executable multi-turn agent tasks. We present a telemetry-to-episode construction method instantiated as BTS-AgentBench. The pipeline normalizes BTS metadata and raw histories into a read-only tool store, compiles static tasks with tool-derived gold answers and evidence, and lifts retained tasks into typed, bounded operator-facing episodes. The 532-row release adds clarification, goal revision, timestamp policy, quality-gated reporting, and evidence attribution while preserving the source computation and split. Coded contract preflight reports zero findings, and the construction-exclusion controller completes 0/532 rows. Two independent raw-to-episode builds match all 11 logical tool-store exports and reproduce the released 356/87/89 train/dev/test artifact exactly. Applying the shared construction path to XAI4HEAT produces 204 episodes; on its 41-row held-out test split, the controller completes 0 rows and the retained GPT-5.5 execution completes all 41. Code, artifacts, and replay reports are available at https://github.com/kjy7567/BTS-AgentBench.

</details>


### [193] [Pair-Level Essay-Scale Republication and Reuse from Fragmented Historical Text Reuse: A Workflow Study on Eighteenth-Century Books and Newspapers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27343)
*Ke Shu,Kira Hinderks,Eetu Mäkelä,Mikko Tolonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将碎片化文本复用证据整合为文章级重印和复用关系的方法，主要以十八世纪哲学家David Hume的文献为例，通过多种方法对比实现证据整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的文本复用检测多关注片段层面的检索，但历史文献的复用往往是零碎和不完整的，需要有效的证据整合以推断文章级的传播和重印关系。

Method: 作者以Hume的文献及其报刊重印为例，设计了三类方法进行候选对证据整合：1）分阶段的规则驱动流程；2）基线方法（决策树和两种大型语言模型直接推断）；3）自动化规则适配。在ECCO数据集上进行标注评测。

Result: 在已知标签的数据上，特征聚合方法F1可达0.948，完整的规则流程取得了最优的查准率-查全率折衷。直接用LLM标注会极大扩大发现数量但查准率低。手工审查ECCO-报刊跨域结果，176个预测均为真实复用，发现了更复杂的文献来源结构。

Conclusion: 针对历史文献碎片化复用的证据，通过可审计的对级证据整合，能有效产生紧凑的候选空间，为历史研究提供实用工具。

Abstract: This paper addresses the recovery of essay-scale republication and reuse from fragmented text-reuse evidence, a setting whose central challenge is pair-level evidence consolidation and not fragment retrieval alone. The study focuses on a candidate set centered on essays by eighteenth-century Scottish philosopher David Hume, spanning books from ECCO (Eighteenth Century Collections Online) and historical newspapers. Because the input consists of fragmented reuse hits instead of clean document pairs, and positive coverage is inherently incomplete, we formulate the task as pair-level evidence consolidation into plausible transmission relations and compare three methodological families: a staged rule-based workflow, baselines (a decision tree and two direct LLM settings), and automated rule adaptation. On labeled ECCO--ECCO slices, pair-level feature aggregation alone already reaches 0.948 F1 on the main labeled slice, while the final workflow gives the strongest overall precision-recall trade-off among the tested rule stages. On the full ECCO--ECCO candidate universe, direct LLM baselines flag up to 14,886 pairs as reprints compared to 771 for the final workflow, behaving in this direct-prompt setup as high-recall candidate expanders rather than precision-controlled deployment classifiers. On ECCO--Newspaper, manual audit confirms all 176 predicted positives as genuine cases of republication or reuse, while issue duplication and source-side multiplicity reveal additional provenance structure. Under incomplete ground truth, auditable pair-level evidence consolidation provides a practical way to produce compact candidate spaces for historical inspection.

</details>


### [194] [INTENT-AS-A-TOOL Makes it Easy to Track Agentic Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27348)
*Yutong Zhang,Jianshuo Dong,Peng Xu,Long Wang,Jie Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Xiaoping Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“INTENT-AS-A-TOOL”的新方法，通过向大模型引入意图工具，更细致地追踪和检测模型潜在的有害动机，其效果优于传统的思维链监控，并有助于更及时地发现和干预危险行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被部署为自治智能体，模型在目标冲突和压力下出现危害性行为的风险增加。现有的思维链（CoT）监控只能粗略反映意图变化，难以及时、细致地识别模型内在动机的变化过程。因此，需要一种更细粒度、实时有效的方法来识别和干预模型的潜在恶意行为。

Method: 作者提出了INTENT-AS-A-TOOL方法，在模型生成过程中嵌入针对意图的工具，允许模型通过调用这些工具显式表达其对某种目标行为的承诺。通过统计模型调用意图工具的概率，可获得无需人工判断、细致连贯的意图变化轨迹。该方法和传统的思维链监控相结合，能够更全面地反映模型的行为倾向和决策动态。

Result: 实验结果表明，INTENT-AS-A-TOOL能够拓展CoT监控，使得对意图的追踪更细致、连贯，且能够识别出模型生成过程中对危险行为产生承诺的关键步骤。该方法在跟踪潜在危害行为和实现在线干预方面显著优于传统做法。

Conclusion: INTENT-AS-A-TOOL提供了一种有效的新途径，可以细致地跟踪和识别自治型大模型在推理过程中的意图偏好，对提升模型的安全性与可控性具有重要意义。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are deployed as autonomous agents, safety failures increasingly involve consequential actions. We study agentic misalignment, where agents take harmful actions under goal conflicts and pressures. Using chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring, we find that harmful execution is often preceded by intent signals in reasoning. However, post-hoc CoT labels are too coarse to show how intent changes during generation. We introduce INTENT-AS-A-TOOL, an approach that adds intent-targeted tools to give the model a dedicated channel for expressing commitment to a target behavior. The probability of calling an intent tool provides a judge-free, fine-grained signal of the model's tendency to pursue that behavior. Our results show that INTENT-AS-A-TOOL complements CoT monitoring, expands post-hoc CoT labels into dense trajectories, and identifies critical steps for online intervention. These findings suggest that action preferences are useful for tracking agentic misalignment during reasoning. Our code and data are accessible: https://github.com/RebeccaZhang22/intent-as-a-tool.

</details>


### [195] [RCMN: Understanding Misleadingness in Influential Public Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27358)
*Peiling Yi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以读者为中心的误导性理解框架（RCMN），并基于此框架构建了权威话语数据集，对主流生成式基础模型在此任务上的表现进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于公众话语中误导性如何产生及其如何影响读者解读的研究较少，主要关注点局限于虚假信息，而忽略了信息的框架、隐去和语境等。本文旨在系统化地理解误导性的多维特征、风险与机制。

Method: 提出了RCMN框架，将‘误导性’操作化为五个维度（机制、读者解读、证据支持、情感唤起与传播意图）；基于此构建带有证据的数据集；利用五种主流生成式模型，通过轻量化主张-语境表示方式，对误导性进行分析与评测。

Result: 实验发现，误导性现象极为多样，不仅限于虚假制造，还常见推理不当、夸大与隐去等机制，且常伴随较高情绪唤起和扭曲性传播意图。轻量级主张-语境表示可有效恢复部分读者层面的误导性解读，但识别具体误导性机制仍有较大挑战。

Conclusion: 轻量级表示适用于大规模误导性分析，但对误导机制的可靠理解仍需更丰富的上下文和证据信息支持。

Abstract: Influential public discourse shapes public beliefs and can also mislead, not only through what is stated, but also through how information is framed, omitted, contextualised, and communicated. Yet less research has focused on how such misleadingness arises and shapes the interpretations formed by readers. To address this gap, we introduce Reader-Centric Misleadingness Understanding (RCMN), a framework that operationalises misleadingness through five dimensions: misleading mechanism, likely reader interpretation, evidence-warranted interpretation, emotional arousal, and communicative intent. Based on this framework, we construct an evidence-grounded dataset of influential public discourse. Empirical findings show that misleadingness is diverse and extends well beyond fabrication, with unsupported inference, exaggeration, and omission among the prevalent mechanisms, and is frequently associated with heightened emotional arousal and distortive communicative intent. Moreover, we investigate whether lightweight claim-and-context representations retain sufficient cues for understanding reader-centric misleadingness without access to richer contextual, evidential, and multimodal information. Evaluation across five recent generative foundation models shows that reader-level interpretations can often be recovered from such limited representations, whereas identifying how misleadingness is produced remains considerably more challenging. These findings highlight the potential of lightweight representations for scalable misleadingness analysis, while reliable understanding of misleading mechanisms continues to require richer contextual and evidential grounding.

</details>


### [196] [Your Voice Cloning System is Secretly a Voice Anonymizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27360)
*Romolo Muletta,Felix Matthias Saaro,Mark Cieliebak,Jan Deriu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用多语言语音克隆模型XTTSv2实现无需重新训练的说话人匿名化方法，并通过迭代优化策略，在多个语种与数据集上取得了高隐私性、可懂度及音质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 说话人匿名化需在保护隐私的同时保持语音内容与质量，现有方法难以兼顾上述需求，且多需面向特定语种训练。

Method: 本研究将XTTSv2模型（27K小时多语言语音训练）用于说话人匿名化，通过伪说话人条件实现语音转换，并提出迭代精炼算法，在说话人可辨识度与语音可懂度的调和均值上优化表现。

Result: 在CommonVoice与Multilingual LibriSpeech七种欧洲语言数据上，系统在保持较高可懂度和远胜基线音质的同时，实现了接近最优的隐私保护（EER约0.49），且无需语言特定训练。

Conclusion: XTTSv2无需重新训练即可实现跨语种高效说话人匿名化，兼顾隐私、内容和语音质量，展现了模型迁移和通用性的潜力。

Abstract: Speaker anonymization suppresses speaker-identifying attributes from speech while preserving linguistic content and quality. We propose repurposing XTTSv2, a multilingual voice cloning model trained on 27k hours of speech, for speaker anonymization without retraining. Our key insight is that XTTSv2's voice cloning capabilities preserve prosodic structure independently of speaker identity, enabling voice conversion by conditioning on a pseudo-speaker. We introduce an iterative refinement strategy that balances privacy and utility by maximizing a harmonic mean of speaker dissimilarity and intelligibility. Evaluated on seven European languages across CommonVoice and Multilingual LibriSpeech, our system achieves near-optimal privacy (EER $\approx$ 0.49), competitive intelligibility, and substantially better speech quality than dedicated anonymization baselines, while requiring no language-specific training. We release the code here: https://github.com/rm00cr/coqui-tts.

</details>


### [197] [Puro-2B: Poor Lab's Qwen2-1.5B Trained on RTX 5090 within $5090](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27370)
*Kairong Luo,Jiarui Cui,Yaorui Yin,Shengqi Chen,Yiming Yang,Linxiang Gao,Yanmohan Wang,Mingzhe Zhang,Kaiyue Wen,Kaifeng Lyu,Wenguang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种面向学术界和开源社区、低成本且易于硬件实现的开源大模型预训练配方，并实际训练了一系列Puro-2B模型，将训练成本从数十万美元降至6900美元左右，模型性能接近Qwen2.5-1.5B，所有训练细节和代码均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流大模型预训练成本极高，对大部分学术与开源群体来说难以负担，即使市面有开源模型和训练流程，低成本、易复现的完整开源训练配方一直缺失。

Method: 作者提出并验证了一套高性价比的预训练全流程，包括选择消费级RTX 5090显卡，采用FP8低精度训练、超球优化、课程模型平均和优化数据流程等创新方法。通过对比各种配方变体，在不同token量预算下训练多组模型。

Result: 在不到6900美元的算力费用下，Puro-2B模型性能接近Qwen2.5-1.5B。同时提出了训练成本与模型性能关系的经验定律（Puro Cost Scaling Law），显示只需约4400美元即可实现和Qwen2-1.5B相近的性能。还系统分析了预训练数据课程对下游任务表现的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的开源大模型训练全流程显著降低了学术界和开源社区的技术和经济门槛，证实了在消费级硬件上实现高性能模型是可行且高效的。论文还推动了可复现实验和更广泛的社区参与，并已开源全部数据、代码和权重。

Abstract: Language model pretraining has become almost synonymous with prohibitive cost, placing it out of reach for much of the academic and open-source communities. Although strong open-source efforts already exist, including open-weight models and open-source training recipes, a cost-efficient, hardware-accessible, and open-source pretraining recipe has long been missing. Even at a small scale, training Llama-3.2-3B costs over \$1.5M, and reproducing SmolLM3-3B needs over \$700K. In this report, we present an open pretraining recipe designed to lower this barrier. Using this recipe, we train a collection of Puro-2B models from scratch on up to 1.4 trillion tokens with FP8 precision on consumer-grade RTX 5090 GPUs. The models in the collection differ in token budgets and selected recipe variants. Our best model is trained at a compute cost of less than \$6.9K and approaches Qwen2.5-1.5B performance under our evaluation protocol. This cost efficiency is enabled by a combination of approaches, including hardware selection, low-precision training, hyperball optimization, curriculum model averaging, and the data recipe. Beyond the recipe itself, we provide two additional results. First, across the Puro-2B collection, we derive a Puro Cost Scaling Law that relates training cost to average model performance; the fitted law suggests that about \$4.4K, less than \$5,090, is sufficient to reach the performance of Qwen2-1.5B. Second, as an end-to-end case study, we examine how pretraining data curricula shape downstream performance after post-training. Such controlled studies are enabled by having access to the full pretraining pipeline rather than model weights alone. We release the full training recipe for Puro-2B, including data, code, and model weights under Apache 2.0 at https://huggingface.co/collections/thu-pacman/puro-2b.

</details>


### [198] [D2C-Routing: Dimension-to-Composition Evidence Routing for Mixed-Origin AI-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27380)
*Xin Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Yiqi Tong,Wei Guo,Yutian Xiao,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于混合来源文本检测的新方法（D2C-Routing），能够区分文本的内容和表达分别由人类或AI生成，从而更准确地识别四种协作类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测方法大多仅能对整篇文档进行二元分类（人类或AI生成），在内容和表达来源不同的混合文本情况下效果较差，因此需要更细致的检测方法。

Method: 作者将检测任务建模为对文本内容和表达两个维度的来源进行归因，然后组合得出四类协作类型。他们提出了D2C-Routing方法，首先分别对内容和表达维度进行特征提取和判别，再通过一个门控组合层整合判别结果，最终输出预测标签。

Result: 在MixD2C数据集（从HART基准重建）上，D2C-Routing检测系统的四分类Avg TPR@1%FPR达0.8603，比基线高6.5点。消融实验验证了方法设计，错误分析显示区分AI内容/人类表达与完全AI生成的文本最有挑战。

Conclusion: D2C-Routing能有效应对AI与人类协作生成文本的检测问题，尤其是在更细粒度区分混合来源时显示出优势，但部分细分类型（AI内容/人类表达）仍需进一步提升区分能力。

Abstract: AI-generated text detection is commonly framed as a binary document-level judgment about whether a text is human-written or machine-generated. This framing breaks down for mixed-origin writing, where content origin and expression origin may differ. We cast mixed-origin detection as dimension-to-composition source attribution, inferring content origin and expression origin before composing them into four collaboration types. We propose Dimension-to-Composition Routing (D2C-Routing), which routes content-side and expression-side evidence to supervised dimension heads before a learned gated composition layer predicts the final label. On MixD2C, a reconstructed split derived from the HART mixed-origin benchmark, our disclosed D2C-Routing-based detector system reaches 0.8603 four-way Avg TPR@1%FPR, 6.5 points above the same-split RACE-local rerun. Core ablations support the routing design, while error analysis shows that distinguishing AI-content/human-expression from fully AI-generated text remains the hardest boundary. Code is available at https://github.com/bystander563/d2c-routing-artifact.

</details>


### [199] [RATIO: A Benchmark for Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations in Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27394)
*Maayan Sharon,Tom Hope*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了RATIO（Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations）基准，旨在支持基于科学文献的灵感检索，通过三种不同的“思维操作”对相关性进行定义。RATIO 基于数百万篇计算机科学文献构建，结合了自动和人工方法用于大规模训练和评估检索系统。实验证明经过操作特定微调后，检索性能提升，但仍有很大改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家的灵感来源于已有文献，但灵感形式多样，包括直接解决方案、抽象扩展或具体实例。现有检索系统难以按不同“灵感操作”精准获取相关内容，缺乏适用于多层次科学灵感检索的大规模评测基准。

Method: 作者构建了RATIO基准，定义三类“思维操作”：Address（为问题检索解决方法），Broaden（检索更一般化问题），Specify（检索具体实例）。通过拓展语篇标记远程监督方法，将其用于大规模检索数据构建，并结合LLM与人工标注完善数据质量。最后，通过在RATIO上微调检索模型进行实验。

Result: 实验结果显示，针对三种操作类别进行专门微调显著提升了检索效果，但整体表现距离理想情况仍有提升空间，表明该任务具有挑战性。

Conclusion: RATIO为研发和评估面向文献驱动科学灵感检索的新系统奠定了基础，推动了科学发现领域检索技术的发展。该基准具备良好的可扩展性，便于后续进行更多改进和深入研究。

Abstract: Retrieved scientific literature can serve as inspiration for both human and AI scientists. Inspiration can take different forms: prior work may directly suggest how to address a problem, or surface directions at different levels of abstraction - zooming out to a more general view or zooming in to a concrete realization. We introduce RATIO (Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations), a large-scale benchmark in which relevance is defined by three operations which we name ideation moves: Address retrieves potential approaches for stated problems, Broaden retrieves more general formulations, and Specify retrieves concrete instantiations. RATIO is constructed from millions of full-text scientific papers across CS literature via a general recipe that extends discourse-marker distant supervision - previously used only for classification - to corpus-scale retrieval, combined with extensive LLM and human vetting. Experiments show that operation-specific fine-tuning substantially boosts retrievers but leaves much room for further improvements. RATIO provides a scalable training and evaluation framework for retrieval components that support literature-grounded ideation, opening up new research avenues on scientific inspiration retrieval.

</details>


### [200] [Making Clinical Language Models Auditable: Concept-Guided Fine-Tuning for Robust Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27397)
*Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的临床文本分类方法CAST，旨在压制模型利用无关文档特征（如模板、格式符号等）带来的性能偏差，实现更具可审计性的模型决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临床语言模型在实际部署时，因过度依赖文本中的无关特征（如模板与固定格式），导致泛化能力下降，需解决模型的可解释性与稳健性问题。

Method: 提出CAST框架，基于稀疏自编码器（Sparse Autoencoder, SAE），将变换器模型的中间特征映射为稀疏、可审计（可被人工理解）的特征。通过LLM辅助解释机制与ICD-10标准，提高特征标记的科学性，并在模型微调阶段对已验证为“文档伪影”的特征进行抑制。同时，模型支持基于每个临床概念的预测解释和追踪。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV住院结局预测任务中，CAST相比经典微调模型有更佳表现，也可与主流大模型持平，同时提供每个预测相关的临床概念及被抑制的无关伪影特征的溯源日志。

Conclusion: CAST不仅提升了模型在临床文本分类中的准确性和泛化能力，还带来了更强的可解释性和审计能力，为临床AI安全部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Clinical language models can achieve strong in-hospital accuracy yet fail under deployment shifts because they exploit note-specific artifacts (e.g., templates, separators, boilerplate) that do not reflect patient state. We propose CAST (Concept-guided Artifact Suppression Tuning), an SAE-based framework for auditable clinical text classification. CAST uses Sparse Autoencoders to expose sparse, human-auditable features from intermediate Transformer activations, labels SAE latents with an LLM-assisted interpretation pipeline and ICD-10 retrieval constraints, suppresses verified artifact latents via residual subtraction during fine-tuning, and provides post-hoc per-concept attributions for auditing model decisions. On MIMIC-IV discharge-note mortality prediction, CAST improves over its corresponding fine-tuned encoder baselines and remains competitive with strong LLM baselines, while producing a feature-level audit trail of the clinical concepts that support each prediction and the artifact concepts suppressed during training.

</details>


### [201] [How Language Models Organize and Structure Moral Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27402)
*Orion Reblitz-Richardson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文分析了大型语言模型（LLM）在组织道德知识时的表现，发现模型能广泛区分不同道德基础，并在其表征空间中以多维方式组织这些知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM可以检测道德内容，但现有研究主要关注于是否能检测到，而未探究模型能否区分出不同的道德基础及其关系，即道德知识的组织结构问题。

Method: 作者基于开放权重的LLM，分别针对道德基础理论（六个维度）训练六个独立的线性探针，分析各探针在模型表征空间中的关系及几何结构，同时与非道德类别做对照。还将方法扩展到道德困境，探查模型如何组合道德基础以表征具体困境。

Result: 六个探针各自对应的方向在模型空间中高度独立但又有正向共有成分（整合特征），且整合性特征在预训练早期即形成，无明显MFT个体化/绑定区分，而反映出训练语料的统计特性。道德困境相关的方向有部分从道德基础组合生成，但主要呈现新的冲突结构，表明模型在表征道德冲突而非给出定论。

Conclusion: LLM展现出了复杂和集成化的道德知识表征结构，不同道德基础既有明显区分又能整合，且道德困境中的冲突本身被模型以高维方式编码，而不是只输出直接结论。

Abstract: How do large language models (LLMs) organize moral knowledge? Models detect moral content broadly, but detection is a low bar. We ask whether they go further, distinguishing moral foundations from one another and organizing the relationships between them geometrically.
  We train six independent linear probes on open-weight language models, one per Moral Foundations Theory (MFT) category (care/harm, fair/cheat, lib/oppress, loy/betray, auth/subv, sanc/degrade), and examine how the resulting directions relate to each other in representation space. We find the directions neither collapse into a single moral detector nor isolate from one another. Rather, they span a near-maximal number of independent dimensions while sharing a positive common component. The shared component is the signature of integration, and it is moral-specific relative to a matched non-moral concept battery built identically (mean pairwise cosine 0.26 vs. 0.013).
  The geometry is consistent across architectures and scale and reaches its integration regime early in pre-training, well before probe accuracy saturates. The structure the model discovers shows no evidence of the individualizing/binding distinction predicted by Moral Foundations Theory (an underpowered test: only 20 candidate partitions exist) but rather reflects corpus statistics. Extending to moral dilemmas, each dilemma direction partially composes from its component foundations, at 2.7x a mismatched-pair baseline, while the majority of its variance encodes conflict-specific structure. The model represents moral tension itself, not a pre-resolved judgment.

</details>


### [202] [Consolidating RLVR Capabilities Across Domains: A Deep Dive into Fusion Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27409)
*Siye Wu,Kai Yang,Yuchen Cai,Xin Xu,Peng-Yuan Wang,Jiaxuan Wang,Jiashun Liu,Jiafei Lyu,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种融合大模型多能力的方法：Merge、Mix RL 和 MOPD，分析了它们的适用场景和优缺点，针对跨领域任务给出实用推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前提升大语言模型多能力时，常需分别训练不同领域专家并再融合。但主流的几种融合范式缺乏系统性比较，不清楚它们的优劣与应用选择。

Method: 组织了Merge（合并专家任务向量）、Mix RL（混合集域数据训练）和MOPD（多教师策略蒸馏）三类融合方法，用统一的专家和数据集、跨模型规模和多领域基准进行比较，分析性能和训练动态。

Result: 三种方法在平均性能上差距很小（最多1.4分），但在个别任务上差异明显（最大8.6分）；表现与任务间相关性相关。每种方法受特定限制：Mix RL依赖任务数据比例，MOPD表现受教师限制，Merge将所有专家更新压缩为一次。三者都提升了单样本准确率，但对解空间覆盖和泛化无大影响。

Conclusion: 提出实际建议：已有专家且追求高效融合时选Merge，无专家且需统一模型时选Mix RL，并调节任务比例；如需保留各领域专家性能则用MOPD。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves specific capabilities of large language models, but covering multiple capabilities often involves training separate domain experts and subsequently consolidating them. We organize three fusion paradigms by the artefacts they reuse: Merge combines expert task vectors, Mix RL pools their datasets, and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) uses both. Because they have largely been studied in isolation, how they compare and how to choose among them remain unclear. We compare all three using shared experts and data across model scales and a multi-domain benchmark suite. Although their average performance differs by at most 1.4 points, the gap reaches 8.6 points on a single benchmark, with domain-level variation tracking cross-domain relations visible in task-vector geometry. Training dynamics expose distinct constraints: Mix RL depends on domain mixture proportions, MOPD remains bounded by its teachers, and Merge compresses all expert updates into one. All three improve single-sample accuracy without measurable gains in solution coverage or losses in held-out capabilities. These results yield a practical guideline: use Merge when experts already exist and cheap fusion is paramount; Mix RL when training a unified model without experts, with domain proportions adjusted for cross-domain transfer; and MOPD when preserving domain-specific gains matters more than surpassing teachers or minimizing end-to-end cost.

</details>


### [203] [Boosting LLM Exploration via Weak-Model Guidance in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27420)
*Xingyu Shen,Huishuai Zhang,Peng Li,Yinchun Wang,Dongyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对RLVR在提升大模型推理能力时引发的生成多样性下降问题，提出了一种通过小模型生成不熟悉前缀以促进探索的新方法，显著提高了推理覆盖率与大k下的pass@k表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然RLVR强化了大模型的推理能力，但造成了策略熵下降，推理路径单一，导致大k下pass@k下降。当前缓解方法多聚焦内部算法调整，未充分利用跨模型外部扰动。作者希望在保障推理正确性的同时提升多样性。

Method: 该方法利用小型、弱性能语言模型生成部分推理轨迹（前缀），要求目标大模型基于这些“不熟悉”的前缀继续完成推理，以打破过度自信，鼓励更多样的推理路径探索，无须额外的SFT或复杂奖励机制。

Result: 通过在多个数学基准上的实验，作者的方案在不同k值下均优于标准RLVR，并且随着k增大优势更加明显，显示出推理路径多样性与覆盖能力的显著提升。

Conclusion: 外部前缀干预可有效抑制策略熵下降，无需复杂设计即可扩展大模型推理路径，提升多样性与覆盖率，适用于提升RLVR训练效能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) significantly improves LLM reasoning but often causes a drop in policy entropy, leading to narrowed reasoning coverage and degraded pass@$k$ for large $k$. While existing methods mitigate this entropy collapse through algorithmic regularizations, cross-model non-parametric perturbation is also neglected. In this work, we propose a simple yet effective approach to preserve the generative diversity of LLMs during RLVR. Instead of relying solely on internal exploration, we force the target model to generate answers based on partial reasoning trajectories generated by a smaller, weaker language models. These unfamiliar prefixes effectively disrupt over-confidence and encourage the exploration of distinct reasoning paths. We empirically study the potential of outer prefixes, revealing the mechanism of the impact of distributional discrepancy to the exploration dynamics in RLVR training. Experiments across multiple mathematical benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms vanilla RLVR. Notably, the performance gain becomes increasingly pronounced as $k$ scales up, demonstrating a substantial expansion of reasoning coverage. Furthermore, our approach efficiently mitigates entropy collapse without requiring additional SFT, intricate reward designs, or complex prompting.

</details>


### [204] [Stochastic Estimation of Transduced Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27428)
*Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson,Samuel Kiegeland,Manuel de Prada Corral,Ryan Cotterell,Tim Vieira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于无放回重采样和逆概率修正的算法，能够更高效且无偏估计跨语言模型（TLM）中目标前缀的概率，并在文本和DNA序列任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLM 需计算所有被转换为特定目标前缀的源字符串的概率和，但这一集合可能指数级大或无限，现有近似法只能获得下界且误差未知，计算与内存开销大。

Method: 用无放回重采样源前缀，然后根据其包含概率加权修正，递归应用得到目标前缀的无偏概率估算。新算法在扩展保留的源前缀时动态采样和减枝，保证算法终止并节省算力。

Result: 该算法在文本和DNA任务对比序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）基线性能更优，文本上计算与方差权衡更佳，DNA上同等粒子数误差更低。将其用于DNA到氨基酸转录概率大幅提升计算速度。

Conclusion: 新算法显著提升了TLM目标前缀概率估计的性能和可扩展性，取代基于阈值裁剪的束和法后，在保持主要结论的同时降低了语料的surprisal估算。

Abstract: Transduced language models (TLMs) compose a pretrained \emph{source} language model with a functional finite-state transducer to induce a language model over \emph{target} strings. Computing the probability of a target prefix under a TLM amounts to summing the source-model probabilities of all source strings that the transducer maps to target strings beginning with that prefix. This set can be exponentially large or infinite. Prior work uses a computational shortcut based on source prefix probabilities, then approximates the resulting sum with threshold-pruned beam summing. This produces a lower bound with unknown error. Instead, we resample source prefixes without replacement and reweight each selected prefix by the inverse of its inclusion probability. We show that applying this correction recursively gives an unbiased estimator of the target prefix probability and lets us estimate the mass lost by threshold pruning. Our beam-summing algorithm extends the retained source prefixes and samples which prefixes to keep, reducing their number as more probability mass is added to the running estimate. This can save computation and guarantees that the run halts with probability one. We evaluate the method on encyclopedic text and DNA against sequential Monte Carlo baselines that resample with replacement. It achieves a better compute--variance tradeoff on text and lower error at the same maximum number of particles on DNA. On a DNA-to-amino-acid transduction, it reduces runtime by several orders of magnitude relative to threshold-pruned beam summing and makes estimating prefix probabilities for long target strings feasible. Replacing threshold pruning with unbiased sampling in a published reading-time analysis substantially lowers the estimated corpus surprisal but leaves the published conclusions unchanged.

</details>


### [205] [TTPO: Test-Time Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27448)
*Aozhe Wang,Zhengxi Lu,Jianze Wang,Shangke Lv,Ying Liu,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Hua Yang,Qianglong Chen,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需标签的测试时训练算法TTPO，在数学推理任务上可媲美有标签方法。其关键在于对伪标签分歧的非对称处理，使模型在缺乏真实标签下仍具备自我优化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型的数理推理依赖强化学习或自蒸馏，但都需要真实标签，难以在测试时自适应。若用多数投票伪标签易被出错误导，亟须更鲁棒的机制。

Method: 作者发现：在伪标签与模型输出分歧时，通常输出错误。为此，提出TTPO方法：对同意分支用OPSD蒸馏，不同意分支用分组RL惩罚，并在Token级加权蒸馏和惩罚。多数路由可提升自监督质量。

Result: 在五大竞赛级数学推理基准上，TTPO（无标签）与传统带标签的OPSD性能持平。Qwen3-1.7B模型TTT准确率从38.0%提升至45.2%，在不“思考”模式下提升25.2%到36.4%，且泛化性良好。

Conclusion: TTPO在无标签测试时，可实现自我提升且效果可与有标签方法媲美，克服了伪标签不准的脆弱性，并展现了强泛化和迁移能力。

Abstract: Recent prominent post-training methods, such as Reinforcement Learning (RL) and On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), have driven rapid progress in mathematical reasoning for large language models, yet their reliance on ground-truth labels precludes test-time training (TTT). Replacing ground truth with majority-vote pseudo-labels is a natural alternative, yet it is fragile: an incorrect vote corrupts the teacher and misleads every token. We observe that this failure mode is asymmetric: rollouts that disagree with the pseudo-label are typically wrong regardless of whether the vote itself is correct. Building on this observation, we propose Test-Time Policy Optimization (TTPO), an asymmetric objective that distills agreeing rollouts via OPSD and penalizes disagreeing rollouts with Grouped RL. Token-level selection further refines both branches: distillation down-weights already-converged positions, while RL penalizes only confident errors. Both updates remain well-grounded even under frequent pseudo-label errors, and majority-vote routing yields tighter self-supervision as the model improves. Without any labels, TTPO matches label-supervised OPSD on five competition-level benchmarks, raises Qwen3-1.7B from 38.0% to 45.2% in TTT, yields +25.2% to +36.4% without thinking, and shows strong cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [206] [CritICL: Inference-Time Weak-to-Strong Generalization from Small Language Model Failure Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27455)
*Yufan Wu,Yinghui He,Zhengyi Hu,Lang Wei,Ruichen Li,Qifan Yang,Ting Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的推理框架CritICL，可高效提升大语言模型推理能力，通过利用较弱模型的失败模式作为示例指导推理，达到更好的效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型推理效率提升方法（如多次生成和外部验证）在提升性能时通常成本较高。作者希望寻找一种在保持高效率的同时还能提升模型推理能力的方法。

Method: CritICL框架借助同一模型家族中较小模型的失败模式，将这些失败模式和对应批判作为上下文示例增强推理。具体包括两种：CritICL-dynamic（自适应预测输入相关失败模式，并检索批判）和CritICL-static（用全局失败模式配置稳定指导推理）。

Result: 实验表明，CritICL在多项推理任务上始终优于标准的in-context learning（上下文学习），并且与（或优于）主流的推理时扩展方法，但仅需更少生成次数和token消耗。

Conclusion: CritICL方法利用失败模式为模型推理提供有效指导，不仅提升了推理表现，同时提高了效率，有望为大模型高效推理开辟新方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in inference-time scaling have significantly improved the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs). However, these methods typically rely on repeated generation or external verification. To address this limitation, we introduce CritICL, a novel inference-time framework that improves reasoning while maintaining high efficiency. Our key insight is that LLM failure modes exhibit structured patterns across model scales within the same family. Instead of treating failures as undesirable outputs, CritICL leverages them as a source of guidance. Specifically, we utilize failure modes derived from weaker models and incorporate them into inference through critique-based in-context examples. We propose two variants: CritICL-dynamic, which adaptively predicts input-specific failure modes and retrieves critiques, and CritICL-static, which uses a global failure mode profile to provide stable guidance. Experimental results show that CritICL consistently outperforms standard in-context learning and achieves performance competitive with or superior to test-time scaling methods, while requiring significantly fewer generations and lower token cost. Code available at: https://github.com/umwyf/CRITICL

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [WALL-SS: Scaling Long-horizon World Models via Next-Scale Autoregression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26239)
*Maeve Zhang,Rain Sun,Xiang Wang,Cyril Zhang,Shalfun Li,Meng Cao,Howard Lu,Ethan Chen,Harry Jhou,KZ Zheng,Lights Shi,Regis Cheng,Lorenzin,Robert Wang,Victor Yao,Gody Li,Elise Mon,Yohann Tang,Ryan Yu,PS Zhang,Vincent Chen,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为WALL-SS的生成式世界模型，通过多尺度自回归缩放方法实现对未来视觉状态的生成，提升机器人在长时间尺度下的推理、模拟和规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式世界模型在机器人领域应用时，存在无法灵活关联动作与结果、支持可变预测跨度与持续交互以及直接进行奖励驱动优化等限制。因此，需提出一种统一的生成建模方式，有效刻画动作-结果因果与长时序模拟能力。

Method: 提出的WALL-SS模型采用多尺度自回归生成方式，将机器人的时空轨迹编码为观测-动作序列因果链。具体包括：（1）动作条件的下一级尺度预测以提升动作与未来场景的耦合; （2）多尺度记忆模块保留近时序的细致信息、远时序进行压缩，同时通过多尺度‘dream forcing’提升自生成过程的稳健性；（3）结合在策略对齐的奖励（action-following，long-term consistency）强化视觉动态预测的一致性。

Result: 实验结果显示，WALL-SS在动作跟随和轨迹准确性方面优于传统方法，可在有限内存下实现分钟级连贯推演，并通过在策略对齐显著降低动作漂移和长时序不一致性。

Conclusion: WALL-SS模型实现了对复杂机器人交互环境的高效、可控和稳健的长时序视觉预测，为下游机器人决策和学习任务提供了更强的世界建模基础。

Abstract: Generative world models provide robots with predictive models of how the world evolves under interaction, with growing potential for simulation, planning, policy evaluation, and robot learning. Beyond clip-level future prediction, a unified generative formulation should relate actions to consequences, support flexible horizons and continuous interaction, and enable reward-driven optimization. We introduce WALL-SS, a world model that generates visual futures through Scale-wise autoregressive Scaling, enabling action-controllable and long-horizon robotic simulation. WALL-SS represents embodied trajectories as causal sequences of temporally interleaved observations and actions, making action-dependent state transitions explicit while naturally supporting variable-length generation, streaming extension through reusable causal states, and direct optimization through sequence probabilities. To make this formulation effective over long horizons, we generate each future observation in a coarse-to-fine manner and develop three complementary components within the same hierarchy. Action-conditioned next-scale prediction injects scale-aligned action representations to improve action-future coupling and model both successful and failed behaviors. Scale-compressed long-horizon memory retains recent interactions at fine resolution while compressing distant observations and actions, with scale-wise dream forcing enhancing robustness to self-generated context. Finally, on-policy alignment optimizes autoregressive visual dynamics with action-following and long-term consistency rewards while preserving the pretrained visual distribution. Experiments show that WALL-SS improves action following and trajectory accuracy, supports coherent minute-long streaming rollout under bounded memory, and consistently benefits from on-policy alignment in reducing action drift and long-horizon inconsistency.

</details>


### [208] [Constraint-Aware Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Static Shape Estimation of Co-Manipulative Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26273)
*Rana Danesh,Pari Qarehdaghi,Farrokh Janabi-Sharifi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种加入物理约束的神经网络（PINN），用于腱驱动共操作柔性机器人系统的静态形状估算。该方法有效提升了估算精度和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性机器人与被操控柔性物体构成的闭环系统，在静力与几何约束下，静态形状估计极其困难，传统方法计算量大且对噪声敏感，因此需要一种高效且物理一致的估算方法。

Method: 作者基于变形几何应变模型，提出了一种结合静力平衡残差和几何闭环残差的PINN，并与纯数据驱动ANN比较，在有限且有噪声的数据集下验证效果。

Result: 在140个样本、50%标签噪声下，PINN分别将配置误差、平衡残差和闭环残差降低约68%、67%和88%；全数据集时相对配置误差仅0.1597%，推断时间远优于传统求解器。实验微调后，标记RMSE降至0.497mm，R2提升至0.937。

Conclusion: PINN方法能够在有限数据和高噪声下，高效、精准且符合物理约束地估算共操作柔性机器人的静态形状，优于传统与纯数据驱动方法。

Abstract: Static shape estimation of co-manipulative continuum robots (CCRs) is challenging because the continuum arms and manipulated flexible object form a closed chain that must satisfy both static equilibrium and geometric loop-closure constraints. This paper presents a constraint-aware physics-informed neural network (PINN) for static shape estimation of a tendon-driven CCR modeled using the geometric variable strain formulation. The proposed method incorporates a projected static equilibrium residual and a configuration-level geometric residual to enforce the governing mechanics and closed-chain geometry. In simulation, the PINN is compared with a purely data-driven artificial neural network (ANN) under limited and noisy training data. With 140 samples and 50% label noise, the PINN reduces the relative configuration error, equilibrium residual, and closed-chain residual by 67.88%, 67.35%, and 88.06%, respectively. Using the full dataset, the PINN achieves 0.1597% relative configuration error with an inference time of 0.1773 ms, compared with 17.97 s for an iterative nonlinear solver. Experimental fine-tuning reduces the marker RMSE from 2.657 mm to 0.497 mm and increases R2 from -0.788 to 0.937. These results demonstrate accurate, physically consistent, and computationally efficient static shape estimation of closed-chain CCRs.

</details>


### [209] [Dispersive Forward Tree Search for Optimal Control: Coverage, Complexity, and Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26314)
*Shashank A. Deshpande,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前向传播的动力学可控路径规划器（DFT*），具备在有限样本下的近最优性理论保证。通过局部分散控制指令集，构建生成用于非线性系统的高效路径搜索树，显著提升了路径规划的效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于steering的方法在处理非线性系统时面临求解状态边界值问题的难题，实际应用受到限制。基于前向传播的方法虽更通用，但缺乏理论性能保证且实现效果有限。因此，亟需设计一种既能回避steering要求，又具备理论保证且实践表现优良的动力学规划方法。

Method: 作者针对非线性系统，特别是微分平坦系统，提出通过前向展开的控制树（DFT*），以分散的控制指令集进行局部空间覆盖。针对控制仿射系统，给出构建高分散性指令集的一般机制。设计了成本剪枝策略，在保证近最优性的同时，将树的规模由指数级降低到多项式级。实现为适于并行处理的广度优先搜索，并为多种典型平台（如单车、拖车、四旋翼）设计高效分散采样器。

Result: DFT*在嵌入式级别处理器上，与现有主流动力学规划器相比，解质量持续有竞争力并常显著更优，且解算时间相近。在并行计算下，还可进一步加速。递推式DFT*可实现在动态环境中的实时规划。

Conclusion: DFT*避免非线性平台中steering的难题，在理论与实际两方面均展现优良性能。为大类动力学系统提供了有限样本下的近最优规划解决方案，具有实际部署和推广价值。

Abstract: Steering-based planners require solutions to state-to-state boundary value problems, which can be inaccessible for nonlinear platforms. Forward propagation evades the steering requirement, but the finite-sample behavior of the associated planners remains uncharacterized and their implementations underperform in practice. This paper develops a propagation-based kinodynamic planner with deterministic finite-sample near-optimality guarantees. We work within the large class of differentially flat nonlinear systems and show that a forward tree of locally dispersive control commands contains a near-optimal trajectory at a certified tree size. We provide a general mechanism to construct dispersive command sets for control-affine systems, which are necessary to implement the search algorithm prescribed by the theory. We show that covering the certified trajectory class irrespective of cost provably demands a tree exponentially sized in the problem horizon, and present a cost-conditioned dominance pruning procedure that retains near-optimality at a tree size polynomial in the horizon. We implement the resulting search algorithm, Dispersive Forward Tree search (DFT*), as breadth-first expansion of the forward tree, which maps naturally onto parallel hardware. We design efficient dispersive samplers for the unicycle, the trailer car, and the quadrotor and evaluate challenging planning tasks for these platforms. DFT* delivers consistently competitive and often substantially better solution quality than state-of-the-art kinodynamic planners at comparable solution times on embedded-tier processors, accelerating further as parallel compute is scaled. We also implement DFT* in a receding-horizon loop to demonstrate real-time planning in dynamic environments at embedded-tier compute budgets.

</details>


### [210] [Cross-Platform Benchmark of Neural 3D Reconstruction for Autonomous Laboratory Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26383)
*Yongho Kim,Mengjiao Han,Victor Mateevitsi,Silvio Rizzi,Michael E. Papka,Nicola Ferrier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了神经3D重建方法在不同算力平台上的实时性能，发现3D高斯溅射相比NeRF有更高渲染质量但耗费更多GPU资源，且轻量级单图重建方法虽快速但会损失细节，建议采用分层处理管线以兼顾效率和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室自动化机器人需要高效、实时的3D重建以辅助物体识别和操作，但现有神经重建方法（如NeRF及其变种）在实际机器人算力平台上的效率和应用效果尚不明晰，急需系统性的性能与效果评估。

Method: 作者对代表性的神经3D重建方法（NeRF、3D Gaussian Splatting与Meta的SAM3D）在多种GPU算力平台（从单板机到服务器）上进行训练与推理基准测试，评估渲染质量、延迟，并对比单幅图像重建与基于场景优化的方法异同。

Result: 3D Gaussian Splatting在渲染质量上优于NeRF，但GPU资源消耗更高；在嵌入式算力上，实现完整逐场景优化难以满足交互式需求。SAM3D虽能在数秒内输出可用几何，但存在细节差异，影响后续操作准确性。

Conclusion: 推荐采用分层神经重建管线：用轻量化重建方法维持实时感知与追踪主循环，复杂高精度重建则择机调度至高性能设备，以兼顾实时性与质量需求。

Abstract: Autonomous robots performing laboratory tasks depend on 3D reconstruction pipelines that can turn raw camera streams into actionable object representations within the latency budget of a physical control loop. Neural 3D reconstruction methods have demonstrated high-quality view synthesis, but their real-time viability across the compute platforms on which laboratory robots actually run remains poorly characterized. In this work, we present a systematic compute-platform benchmark of neural 3D reconstruction methods, evaluating NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting training and rendering on GPU-enabled computing devices ranging from single-board computers to server-class nodes, and place Meta's SAM3D single-image reconstruction on the same axes to quantify its latency and fidelity gap relative to per-scene optimization. Our results show that Gaussian Splatting yields higher rendering quality than NeRF at greater GPU cost, and that onboard compute is insufficient for full per-scene optimization at interactive rates. Our preliminary assessment on SAM3D indicates that it delivers plausible object geometry within seconds, but with detail mismatches that can compromise downstream manipulation. Together, these findings motivate tiered pipelines in which lightweight feed-forward reconstruction sustains the real-time perception-and-tracking loop for laboratory robots, while heavier neural reconstruction is scheduled selectively on suitable compute.

</details>


### [211] [RTNav: Towards Real-Time Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26496)
*Easop Lee,Lingyu Zhang,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 现有导航系统通常忽视了推理延时问题，导致在现实时间约束下性能下降。本文提出RTNav，显式考虑推理延迟和异步环境步进，有效提升实时导航能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉与语言大模型的导航方法，在模拟器中设计时忽略了推理耗时，但现实世界中推理延迟会严重影响任务表现。因此，急需兼顾实时性的架构设计。

Method: 提出了RTNav架构，将推理延迟、异步环境步进和计算资源限制作为核心设计要素，以提升系统在真实时间约束下的表现。

Result: 在HM3D-v1、HM3D-v2和HM3D-OVON的实时版本上，RTNav相比现有方法在成功率上提高了最高11%，在按完成时间加权的成功率（Success weighted by Completion Time）上提升了5.1个百分点。

Conclusion: RTNav有效地缓解了因推理延迟带来的性能下降，可在现实世界导航任务中显著提升机器人实时性能，强调了在实际部署中显性考虑延迟和算力约束的重要性。

Abstract: Navigation in unknown environments to find unforeseen objects has become increasingly feasible with capable vision and language foundation models. However, these models also introduce non-negligible inference latency, which becomes an important concern when agents must operate continuously in the real world. Most state-of-the-art methods are still developed in synchronous simulators, where the environment waits for the agent to act and inference time is effectively free. As a result, agents are often designed around the sequential execution of perception, reasoning, and action, with little regard for time constraints. Under real-time execution, where wall-clock time counts towards the task budget, the inefficiencies of these architectures become clear. We show that recent zero-shot object navigation methods suffer consistent performance degradation under such realistic timing conditions. Motivated by this observation, we propose RTNav, a simple but effective architecture that treats inference latency, asynchronous environment stepping, and bounded compute as explicit design considerations. Evaluated on real-time variants of HM3D-v1, HM3D-v2, and HM3D-OVON, RTNav improves the success rate by up to 11% and the Success weighted by Completion Time by up to 5.1 points over prior work.

</details>


### [212] [Closing the Loop on the Poppy Humanoid: Bipedal Locomotion with Linear-Quadratic Control and Learned Cost Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26505)
*Xulin Chen,Borui He,Ruipeng Liu,Naveed Tahir,Zhenyu Gan,Garrett E. Katz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于LQR的Poppy Humanoid双足机器人闭环行走控制器，实现了在标准硬件上的稳定、无辅助行走，并通过实验验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Poppy Humanoid是开源低成本人形机器人，有探索AI的潜力，但缺乏可靠的双足行走方法。传统的开环控制在运动可靠性上表现不佳，亟需开发更高效的闭环控制方案。

Method: 作者采用线性-二次型调节器（LQR）框架设计闭环运动控制器。首先通过开环播放步行轨迹收集数据，然后学习出LQR控制器所需的二次代价函数，提升行走轨迹跟踪的稳定性和可靠性。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的闭环控制器在Poppy机器人上显著提升了行走的稳定性与可靠性，相较于原始开环方式表现更优，且具备统计学意义上的改进。

Conclusion: 提出的方法为Poppy Humanoid机器人实现无辅助稳定双足行走提供了有效解决方案，有助于促进该机器人在AI领域科研与教育中的应用推广。

Abstract: The Poppy Humanoid is an open-source, low-cost robot suitable for research and education in artificial intelligence. However, we are unaware of any published methodology that achieves reliable, unassisted bipedal locomotion on the standard Poppy hardware. This paper contributes a functional closed-loop walking controller for Poppy, based on the linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) framework for trajectory tracking. Starting with data collected from open-loop playback of a nominal walking trajectory, our proposed method learns a quadratic cost function for an LQR controller that substantially improves the reliability of the motion. The closed-loop controller is validated empirically, demonstrating statistically significant improvements in walking performance compared to open-loop trajectory playback.

</details>


### [213] [SOLO: Stable Omni-terrain Long-Horizon Perceptive Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26583)
*Pihai Sun,Gang Han,Jingkai Sun,Jiahao Ma,Zeran Su,Zelin Tao,Peiran Liu,Shuai Shi,Wei Cui,Zifan Wang,Jialin Yu,Wen Zhao,Kangning Yin,Jiaxu Wang,Jiahang Cao,Lingfeng Zhang,Hao Cheng,Jian Tang,Yijie Guo,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SOLO框架，可显著提升类人机器人在复杂地形中长距离行走的稳定性，其核心在于精准地形重建与改进的蒸馏方法，使机器人能够稳健穿越极端地形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人在复杂地形中由于感知和控制误差累积，长距离移动时容易失去平衡。现有方法常因地形信息重建模糊和模仿方法无法有效分配时序责任，导致鲁棒性下降。

Method: 提出SOLO统一框架，包括两大创新：(1) Query Reconstructor（QR）：用傅里叶编码在体素格上提取细粒度地形特征，显著提升地形还原精度，保留地形边界。(2) Trajectory-Aware MSE（TA-MSE）蒸馏：将师生下一个状态的不一致性作为PPO奖励，还用Generalized Advantage Estimation将未来奖励处罚回溯到前序动作。

Result: 仿真实验表明：QR地形重构L1误差降低至基线的3.3-4.0倍，TA-MSE在任务学习进度上优于PPO与MSE+PPO。在复杂地形压力测试中，SOLO通过率为97.5%，踩点过关率96%，大幅超过其他方法（75.0-75.6%，0-3%）。实际部署中，单凭胸部深度相机与本体传感，SOLO完成了1.5公里户外路段和室内混合地形测试。

Conclusion: SOLO框架有效克服感知与控制误差累积问题，实现类人机器人高精度、长距离地形穿越，在仿真和现实环境中均展现出极高鲁棒性。

Abstract: Humans traverse complex terrain over long distances without losing balance, whereas perceptive humanoid policies become fragile as perception and control errors accumulate. We present SOLO, a unified framework addressing two compounding causes of this long-horizon fragility: dense terrain reconstruction smooths action-critical details, and pointwise imitation lacks temporal credit assignment. Its Query Reconstructor (QR) uses Fourier-encoded cell queries to retrieve spatially specific evidence from depth-proprioception tokens, preserving sharp terrain boundaries. Trajectory-Aware MSE (TA-MSE) Distillation adds next-state teacher-student disagreement to the PPO reward, enabling Generalized Advantage Estimation to propagate future disagreement penalties to preceding actions. In simulation, QR reduces height-map L1 error by factors of 3.3-4.0, while TA-MSE surpasses PPO and MSE+PPO in curriculum progression. On stress-test terrains, SOLO achieves 97.5% mean traversal success and 96% stepping-stone success, versus 75.0-75.6% and 0-3% for dense-reconstructor variants. Deployed zero-shot with only a chest-mounted depth camera and proprioception, SOLO completes a continuous 1.5-km outdoor route and an indoor mixed-terrain course. Project page: https://sunpihai-up.github.io/solo/

</details>


### [214] [Relaxation-Aware Multimodal Sensing of Soft Gripper Driven by Structure-Perception-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26622)
*Yanzhe Wang,Hao Wu,Ziyi Zheng,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合机构设计、感知与学习的软体机器人抓握系统，能够实时感知与补偿因粘弹性导致的抓握力衰减，实现了稳定持久的抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器手因其顺应性被广泛应用于安全、适应性接触场景，但其固有的粘弹性材料会造成抓握力随时间衰减，难以实现长期稳定抓取。如何在保持柔顺性的同时补偿这一缺陷，是实现实用软体抓手的核心难题。

Method: 设计了可变刚度的软体夹爪，集成视觉与红外热成像实现实时变形和温度场追踪；提出与温度相关的粘弹性力模型和基于物理的学习方法，能够重建力随时间的变化趋势并实时补偿因材料松弛带来的抓力流失。

Result: 在280秒的受控抓握任务中，系统抓握力的均方误差为0.066N，较固定开口和单次控制基线方法分别提升了80%和95%。

Conclusion: 结构（机构）与AI协同设计可实现软体手中力的稳定可控：机构限制交互空间，AI学习并补偿粘弹性不确定性，共同实现了长期稳定的抓取。

Abstract: Achieving stable, sustained grasping with soft robotic hands remains a fundamental challenge. Compliance enables safe and adaptive contact, yet the intrinsic viscoelasticity of soft polymers leads to stress relaxation and a continuous decay of grasping force during holding. Inspired by human grasping, which combines phase-dependent stiffness regulation with continuous sensing and feedback, this paper presents an integrated structure--perception--learning framework. We develop a variable-stiffness soft gripper that uses onboard vision and infrared thermography to track deformation and the temperature field in real time, preserving continuous tracking of the interaction state. To mitigate relaxation-induced force decay, we propose a temperature-coupled viscoelastic force representation, together with a physics-informed learning model, to reconstruct the force trend and provide explicit compensation during holding. Experiments show that, in a 280s force-controlled grasp-and-hold task, the proposed method maintains the desired force with a mean absolute error of 0.066N, outperforming fixed-aperture and instantaneous-only baselines by 80% and 95%, respectively. Overall, the results support a mechanism--AI co-design view: mechanisms shape feasible interactions, while learning compensates remaining uncertainty in viscoelastic dynamics, together enabling stable, sustained grasping.

</details>


### [215] [FLARE: A Failure-Aware Framework for Autonomous Correction and Recovery in Visual-Language Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26645)
*Ganlong Zhao,Zijia Tang,Xingping Chen,Zhanghui Kuang,Ye Tian,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FLARE框架，为视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）引入了“重试”和“重置”机制，实现机器人操作中的稳健错误恢复，显著提升了任务成功率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA在机器人长时操作任务中表现优秀，但由于主要依赖于无错误示范进行训练，导致其在遇到抓取失败、物体掉落或意外碰撞时，缺乏自我恢复能力，实际应用鲁棒性差。

Method: 提出FLARE框架：(1) 通过向示范中注入扰动和桥接片段，实现“重试”，使策略能自主处理执行偏差；(2) 利用多模态大模型（MLLM）分析执行视频，自动识别环境越界（OOD）状态，针对性地收集并训练“重置”技能库，用于恢复到有效任务状态。推理阶段由MLLM监控任务执行与“重置”技能的切换。

Result: 在多个具挑战性的复杂接触操作任务上，实验结果表明FLARE显著提升了任务的成功率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FLARE通过“重试”和“重置”机制，有效弥补了现有VLA模型错误恢复能力的不足，大幅提升了机器人操作的实际可用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action Models~(VLAs) have demonstrated significant promise in generalizing to complex, long-horizon robotic manipulation tasks. However, their performance remains brittle, as they are typically trained on trajectory-monotonic, failure-free demonstrations. This reliance on ``perfect" data leaves them unable to recover from common execution errors, such as a missed grasp, a dropped object, or an unexpected collision. In this paper, we propose FLARE, a novel framework that endows VLAs with robust error recovery capabilities through a ``Retry" and ``Reset" paradigm. First, we introduce a ``Retry" mechanism by injecting perturbation and bridging segments that decouple robot pose from environment state into demonstrations, enabling the policy to autonomously handle execution deviations. Second, to address critical, state-breaking (OOD) failures, we introduce a ``Reset" pipeline. We leverage an MLLM for offline failure analysis to automatically identify OOD states from execution videos. This analysis enables the efficient, targeted collection of a small library of object-centric ``Reset" skills, which are trained to restore the environment to a task-valid state. Our full framework integrates these learned policies. At inference, an online MLLM monitor arbitrates between task execution and ``Reset" skills. Experiments on challenging, contact-rich manipulation tasks show our approach significantly improves task success and robustness.

</details>


### [216] [Beyond the Proving Ground: Independent Public-Road Testing of Assisted Lane Change Systems using LiDAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26669)
*Marcello Cellina,Akos Kriston,Antonio Migneco,Davide Maggi,Stefano Favelli,Fabrizio Re,Fabrizio Minarini,Andrea Nuovo,Riccardo Dona,Biagio Ciuffo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可在公共道路上独立测试高级辅助换道系统（ALC）的新方法，并通过A31法国高速公路实地测试验证其有效性。结果显示部分ALC系统存在不符合法规的行为，可为监管和标准制定提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前辅助驾驶系统多在试验场内测试，但这无法完全反映真实驾驶复杂性，且地理围栏限制、需厂家合作影响测试独立性，因此需发展可独立、真实场景下的测试方法。

Method: 研究团队设计并执行了A31高速公路的实地测试，采用配备激光雷达的检测跟踪系统的测试车，根据不同的车距和速度组合进行换道动作测试，并利用高精度GNSS评估检测及跟踪表现，所记录轨迹与UNECE第79号法规要求进行对比分析。

Result: 27种预设换道动作中完成18次，9次主动抑制。6次换道未达到法规规定的最小安全距离，3次超标现象即便考虑测量误差后仍然显著。

Conclusion: 该方法首次实现在公共道路上针对法规安全距离要求的辅助换道合规性测试，证明了激光雷达技术的适用性，并为监管和法规完善提供依据，弥补了现有测试方法的不足。

Abstract: Testing of commercial Advanced Driver Assistance Systems is essential to ensure safety and compliance during type approval and in service operation. However, proving ground scenarios may not reflect real world driving complexity, while geo fencing can require manufacturer collaboration and limit assessment independence. This work presents a methodology for independently testing Assisted Lane Change systems on public roads. A campaign on the A31 French motorway used a test vehicle equipped with a LiDAR based vehicle detection and tracking system. Tests covered combinations of inter vehicle distance and speed between the test vehicle and the take over vehicle. Real time kinematic global navigation satellite system receivers assessed detection and tracking performance. Recorded lane change trajectories were compared with the lane change suppression requirements of UNECE Regulation Number 79. Of 27 predefined lane change manoeuvres, 18 were completed and 9 suppressed. In 6 cases, the system allowed manoeuvres that did not meet regulatory minimum distance requirements. In 3 cases, the deviation remained statistically significant after accounting for measurement uncertainty. To the authors knowledge, this is the first public road campaign designed to assess Assisted Lane Change compliance with Regulation Number 79 safety distance requirements. The results demonstrate the suitability of LiDAR based sensing for this purpose. The methodology can support market surveillance and future regulatory revisions by revealing real world behaviours not covered by approval procedures.

</details>


### [217] [PredVLA: A Sub-Million-Parameter Predictive-Coding Policy for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26673)
*Hiroki Sawada,Shunichi Kasahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种仅有0.68M参数、无需机器人数据预训练的语言条件预测编码控制策略PredVLA，在LIBERO基准测试上展现出与大模型可比的操控性能，并能明确区分观察反馈带来的实际改正效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流视觉-语言-动作模型参数量巨大，尚不确定更小规模、结构差异显著的控制架构能否在参数量有限的情况下实现有竞争力的表现。

Method: 提出PredVLA，一种分层生成递归预测编码策略：输入语言，递归预测视觉特征和本体感觉，系统状态仅通过观测与预测误差的在线推断更新；且完全不依赖机器人数据预训练。采用统一的前端、示范、动作解码和评估流程与LSTM及Transformer对照。

Result: 在LIBERO短时任务平均成功率86.9%，加入长时任务为75.4%。在参数量相同的条件下，比Transformer高3.7倍、LSTM高7.4倍。基于预测误差的闭环结构使得观测对状态改正的作用可明确量化，通过禁用观测推断可做开环基线对比。

Conclusion: 子百万参数的递归生成模型在现代语言条件操作任务中同样能有强大表现，并为预测误差驱动的在线状态修正机制提供了贯彻和评估的方法。

Abstract: Large pretrained vision-language-action models dominate modern robot-manipulation benchmarks, but it remains unclear how much model scale is necessary for strong language-conditioned control, or whether fundamentally different control architectures can remain competitive at much smaller parameter budgets. We present PredVLA, a language-conditioned predictive-coding policy with only 0.68 million trainable network parameters and no robot-data pretraining, whose hierarchical generative recurrent dynamics predict visual features and proprioception while observations influence latent state only through online inference from the resulting sensory prediction errors. On LIBERO, PredVLA achieves an 86.9% mean success rate across the three short-horizon suites and 75.4% when the long-horizon suite is included. Under a controlled comparison using the same frozen front end, demonstrations, action decoder, and evaluation protocol, PredVLA achieves 3.7x and 7.4x mean success rates of parameter-matched Transformer and LSTM policies, respectively. The predictive-coding formulation also makes the contribution of observation-driven correction directly measurable: because observations influence the recurrent state only through prediction-error-based latent inference, disabling this inference yields an exact open-loop control condition. Together, these results show that a sub-million-parameter recurrent generative policy can achieve strong performance on modern language-conditioned manipulation benchmarks while providing an explicit mechanism for prediction-error-driven online state correction.

</details>


### [218] [Residual Deep Reinforcement Learning-Based Computed Torque Control for a Cable-Driven Lower-Limb Rehabilitation Robot under Disturbances and Parametric Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26739)
*Mohammad-Hossein Fakouri,Ali Keymasi-Khalaji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合残差深度强化学习的计算力矩控制方法，用于提升下肢康复机器人在各类不确定和受扰动情况下的轨迹跟踪性能，同时保障可解释性和约束合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模型控制方法虽具有良好可解释性，但对于模型不确定性和扰动很敏感；而纯学习方法透明度低、难以处理物理约束。该工作旨在在二者之间找到平衡，有效提升康复机器人轨迹跟踪的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了残差深度强化学习增强的计算力矩控制框架：用计算力矩法产生主控制命令，再由有界的深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）学习器提供补偿力矩。通过仿真在多种状态和工况下，以及多项性能指标（轨迹、关节极限、电缆约束等）对该方法进行评估。

Result: 残差控制方法相较于单独的计算力矩控制，能显著提升轨迹跟踪和扰动抑制能力，同时保持模型可解释性并满足约束检查。实验还发现此法的提升具有一定广泛性，但部分情况下受限于轨迹本身约束。

Conclusion: 有界残差强化学习可作为提升康复机器人仿真控制鲁棒性的有效策略，建议后续开展更全面的约束感知和实验验证研究。

Abstract: Accurate trajectory tracking in cable-driven lower-limb rehabilitation robots is challenging because model uncertainty, external disturbances, joint constraints, and pull-only cable actuation can degrade nominal control performance. Conventional model-based controllers provide an interpretable control structure but remain sensitive to model mismatch, whereas fully learning-based control can reduce transparency and complicate constraint-aware operation. This study proposes a residual deep reinforcement learning-enhanced computed torque control framework in which computed torque control generates the nominal command and a bounded Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient policy supplies only an additional compensating torque. The approach is evaluated in simulation under nominal, uncertain, disturbed, combined, and generalization conditions, together with trajectory-tracking, joint-limit, cable-demand, workspace-feasibility, and cable-Jacobian diagnostics. Across the evaluated conditions, the residual controller improves tracking and disturbance rejection relative to computed torque control while preserving the interpretable model-based command structure and satisfying the reported feasibility checks in the representative evaluation. Broader tests indicate that tracking improvements can persist beyond the representative case while also exposing trajectory-dependent constraint limitations. These results support bounded residual learning as a practical robustness-enhancement strategy for simulation-based rehabilitation robot control and motivate further constraint-aware and experimental validation.

</details>


### [219] [MeshPriorDiT: Hierarchical Modeling for Action-Conditioned Cloth Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26766)
*Zihang Wang,Jianming Hu,Shang Su,Hao Huang,Mengkai Shi,Jun Gao,Shuo Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了MeshPriorDiT，一种结合结构化网格先验与生成式残差的层次化模型，有效提升了布料动力学预测的局部与全局协调能力。实验结果表明，该方法在多个布料操作任务中显著优于传统GNN与Transformer模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有布料运动预测方法各有优劣：基于网格的GNN关注局部反应但难以处理远距离协调，基于Transformer的模型虽能捕捉长距离交互但缺乏物质拓扑先验。如何兼顾局部物理准确性与全局协调性是关键挑战。

Method: 本文提出MeshPriorDiT。首先采用动作条件化网格GNN预测多步顶点位移构造参考轨迹，体现材料拓扑结构及抓取约束；随后，基于历史状态、动作和网格先验，利用残差生成器（Residual DiT）进行条件流匹配，生成尚未被先验捕捉到的残差运动，并利用材料邻接关系协调修正。

Result: 在三种布料操作任务上进行15步自回归预测，MeshPriorDiT相比GNN-Only基线平均降低43.42%的全局MSE，相比DiT-DDPM降低75.03%，且边应变MSE表现与GNN-Only相当。

Conclusion: MeshPriorDiT能够有效结合局部物理响应和全局拓扑协调，提升布料动力学预测的准确性与稳定性，是比单一GNN或Transformer更优的方案。

Abstract: Action-conditioned cloth dynamics prediction requires both locally plausible deformation and long-range coordination. Existing approaches largely follow two paradigms.
